### [173] [AgenticER: the next frontier in Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27435)
*George Papadakis,Panos Korovesis,Manolis Koubarakis,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将实体解析（ER）从传统的“被动范式”转向“智能体驱动（Agentic ER）”的新范式。通过构建能主动规划策略、收集证据并权衡成本与精度的自主智能体，解决数据湖中流式异构数据的模糊性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ER方法多基于静态相似度计算，属于“被动范式”，无法处理现实世界中数据的不确定性和上下文依赖性，特别是在包含CSV、JSON、RDF及自由文本等异构流式内容的复杂场景下，难以通过单次计算解决歧义。

Method: 1. 提出Agentic ER概念，将ER建模为序列决策过程；2. 设计自主智能体架构，使其能主动制定策略、获取外部证据、决定是否需要查询额外来源或人工介入；3. 将其形式化为决策理论问题，提出参考架构，识别核心研究挑战，并定义针对智能体行为的新评估维度。

Result: 建立了Agentic ER的理论框架和参考架构，明确了在准确性、成本和延迟之间的优化机制，并提出了新的评估标准，填补了动态、交互式实体解析的研究空白。

Conclusion: Agentic ER代表了数据管理与智能代理交叉领域的新研究方向，通过引入主动决策和迭代推理机制，显著提升了复杂异构数据环境下的实体解析能力。

Abstract: Entity Resolution (ER) is a fundamental problem in data management, playing a critical role in tasks like data cleaning and knowledge graph construction. The existing ER approaches range from traditional rule-based to deep learning techniques and LLM-based methods, but typically operate under a ``passive paradigm'', as duplicates are detected through static, one-shot similarity computations. Such approaches fail to capture the inherently uncertain and context-dependent nature of real-world ER tasks, especially in data lakes with streaming content in heterogeneous formats such as CSV files, JSON files, RDF dumps, and free text. In such settings, resolving ambiguity often requires iterative evidence gathering, reasoning across multiple sources, even selective human involvement. To cover this gap, we advocate a paradigm shift from passive to Agentic ER, which frames ER as a sequential decision-making process that is performed by autonomous agents. These agents actively plan ER strategies, acquire external evidence, decide when to query additional sources or humans, and optimize trade-offs between accuracy, cost, and latency. We formalize Agentic ER as a decision-theoretic problem, we propose a reference architecture, we identify core research challenges, and outline new evaluation dimensions tailored to agentic behavior. By introducing Agentic ER, we aim to establish a new research direction at the intersection of data management and intelligent agents.

</details>


### [174] [Fully Inductive Cardinality Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28311)
*Tim Schwabe,Lukas Ketzer,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出FICE，首个无需重训练即可泛化到完全未见知识图谱的BGP查询基数估计器。通过因子图视图的GNN编码器生成嵌入，结合解码器预测对数基数，在10个KG上显著降低中位数q-error（从13.54降至5.34），且延迟低于毫秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型基数估计器虽优于统计和采样方法，但具有归纳偏置限制（transductive），需随图谱变化或应用于新图谱时重新训练，阻碍其在真实世界三元组存储中的应用。

Method: 构建FICE模型：1) 基于因子图视图的编码器GNN，利用2-hop邻域局部消息传递机制生成实体和关系嵌入；2) 解码器GNN沿查询连接拓扑组合嵌入以预测对数基数；3) 联合训练编码器和解码器，使用邻域采样扩展至百万级三元组规模，解耦嵌入生成与基数解码。

Result: 在10个知识图谱上的实验表明，FICE将整体中位数q-error从最佳竞争者的13.54降低至5.34，并在尾部行为表现上优于所有对比方法（包括学习型和非学习型基线）。

Conclusion: FICE实现了完全归纳式的基数估计，无需重训练即可泛化至未见过图谱及关系，兼具高精度、低延迟和可扩展性，解决了现有学习方法在动态图谱环境中的适用性问题。

Abstract: Query optimization of Basic Graph Patterns (BGP) SPARQL queries over Knowledge Graphs (KG) requires accurate cardinality estimation. Recently published learned estimators outperform statistics- and sampling-based approaches, but share a limitation preventing their adoption in real-world triplestores: they are transductive and require retraining when the underlying graph changes or when applied to new graphs. We present FICE (Fully Inductive Cardinality Estimation), the first learned cardinality estimator for BGP queries over KGs that generalizes to entirely unseen graphs (including unseen relations), without any retraining. FICE is a graph neural network (GNN) with two coupled components. First, an encoder GNN over a factor-graph view of the KG produces entity and relation embeddings. We prove that BGP cardinality is a local function of the 2-hop neighborhood around bound terms in this view, motivating the local message-passing encoder. A decoder GNN then composes these embeddings along the join topology of the query to predict log-cardinality. The encoder and decoder are trained jointly, making the embeddings specialized for cardinality estimation. FICE is trained using neighborhood sampling to scale to KGs with millions of triples, and decouples embedding generation from cardinality decoding to enable estimation latency below a millisecond. Compared to learned and non-learned baselines over 10 KGs, FICE reduces the overall median q-error from 13.54 (for the best competitor) to 5.34 and dominates all approaches in tail behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [OneShot: Index-in-Ranking with Neural Scoring for Large-Scale Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27475)
*Ziwei Li,Shuyao Li,Xufeng Cai,Xue Zou,Yiming Ma,Huiting Lu,Wujie Yan,Zhichen Zhao,Yang Lu,Zhe Wang,Rui Luo,Zhengyu Su,Dan Zhang,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OneShot框架，通过端到端联合学习解决推荐系统中检索阶段的排序目标与索引结构对齐问题，在Instagram短视频推荐中显著提升了召回率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统的检索阶段需从数十亿候选项中筛选出数千项，传统上排序准确性（优化预测与用户行为的一致性）和索引效率（优化物品表示的结构化分组以实现快速搜索）这两个目标是错位的，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 提出OneShot，一种端到端的模型内索引学习框架，原生地将索引学习与排序目标对齐；利用联合学习作为结构基础，扩展交互建模能力，突破传统点积瓶颈，使用神经评分进行更复杂的交互建模。

Result: OneShot已在Instagram工业级短视频推荐系统中全量部署，显著提升用户日均会话数、互动量和停留时间；在运营排名体积下实现20%的召回率提升，且在同等召回水平下效率提升10倍。

Conclusion: OneShot有效解决了排序与索引之间的长期对立问题，通过统一学习框架实现了检索表达能力和效率的双重突破，具有显著的工业应用价值。

Abstract: In modern recommendation systems, retrieval serves as a primary stage responsible for filtering billions of candidate items down to thousands prior to refined ranking. To make this massive search effective and efficient, the system relies on ranking accuracy and indexing efficiency. However, these two objectives are traditionally misaligned: while the former optimizes for the alignment between ranking predictions and user behavior, the latter optimizes for a structural grouping of item representations which enables fast search among billions of candidates. Thus, despite extensive efforts to scale up interaction modeling for retrieval, they remain fundamentally limited by the structural misalignment between the ranking objectives and the proximity-learned index. In this work, we address this long-standing dichotomy by proposing a new holistic retrieval framework, OneShot. It is an end-to-end, in-model index learning framework that natively aligns index learning with ranking objectives. Using this joint learning as a structural foundation, OneShot pushes the boundaries of retrieval expressiveness by scaling interaction modeling with neural scoring beyond the persistent dot-product bottleneck. OneShot is fully deployed in Instagram's industrial short-video recommendation system, driving significant wins in user daily sessions, engagement, and time-spent. Additionally, OneShot achieves a $20\%$ recall gain at the operational ranking volume and a 10x efficiency improvement at an equivalent recall level.

</details>


### [176] [Hierarchical Reranking for Scalable Financial RAG System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27523)
*Joohyun Lee,Sungwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Hierarchical Reranker的RAG框架，旨在解决金融文档（如10-K文件、表格披露）中混合文本-表结构处理和大规模数据检索的难题。通过预检索优化、分层重排序架构和长上下文管理三大创新，显著提升了检索性能和生成可靠性，在FinQA等基准测试中表现优异，并在ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成（RAG）系统在处理金融文档时面临两大挑战：一是难以有效处理混合文本-表格结构；二是面对海量金融文档时处理能力不足。此外，金融分析需要专家级推理能力，而现有系统往往缺乏足够的准确性和效率来满足这一需求。

Method: 提出了Hierarchical Reranker框架，包含三个核心创新：
1. 预检索优化（Pre-Retrieval Optimization）：通过标准化、关键词扩展和表格转换提升查询清晰度和搜索效率。
2. 分层重排序架构（Hierarchical Reranker Architecture）：采用两阶段排序机制提高检索精度。
3. 长上下文管理（Long-Context Management）：通过自适应输入分区和融合技术，在超长上下文中保持推理准确性。

Result: 在FinQA、FinanceBench和ConvFinQA等多个基准测试中，该系统取得了0.7918的NDCG@20分数，并展现出优越的事实一致性。其鲁棒性在ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge中得到验证，获得第二名。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可部署、领域优化的RAG管道，显著提高了金融推理的准确性和可扩展性，为自动化审计报告和量化投资分析奠定了基础。源代码将在论文被接受后公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Analyzing financial documents such as 10-K filings, tabular disclosures, and macroeconomic reports demands expert reasoning and extensive time. However, existing Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems often struggle to process hybrid text-table structures or the massive scale of financial documents. To address these challenges, we propose Hierarchical Reranker, a RAG framework designed to improve retrieval performance and generative reliability across large-scale financial datasets. The system integrates three key innovations: Pre-Retrieval Optimization, enhancing query clarity and search efficiency through normalization, keyword expansion, and table transformation; Hierarchical Reranker Architecture, improving retrieval precision through a two-stage ranking mechanism; and Long-Context Management, preserving reasoning accuracy through adaptive input partitioning and fusion under extensive contexts. Across multiple benchmarks, including FinQA, FinanceBench, and ConvFinQA, the proposed system achieved an NDCG@20 score of 0.7918 and demonstrated superior factual consistency. Its robustness was further validated by achieving second place in the ACM-ICAIF '24 FinanceRAG Challenge. This work presents a deployable, domain-optimized RAG pipeline that enhances both the accuracy and scalability of financial reasoning, paving the way for automated audit reporting and quantitative investment analysis. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub upon acceptance.

</details>


### [177] [Heterogeneous Ranking in Industrial-Scale Recommender Systems: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27577)
*Di Bai,Jintao Liu,Zhenwei Tang,Peifan Wu,Nada Al-Thawr,Luoshu Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Google Discover中用于处理异构推荐内容（如文章、视频、UGC等）的工业级多任务排序方案。通过引入HA-MoE架构和LENS可观测性框架，并结合DL-AUC评估指标，有效解决了异构环境下的负迁移和多数类偏见问题，显著提升了排序性能和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构推荐信息流（如Google Discover）整合了来自开放网络的多样化内容，不同内容类型具有独特的特征密度和用户交互模式。构建一个能在保持高性能的同时避免负迁移或多数类偏见的统一排序模型，是工业界面临的重大挑战。

Method: 1. 提出HA-MoE架构：一种异构自适应的多门控混合专家网络，将显式的异构上下文融入门控网络和专家表示中，实现有效专业化且不显著增加运营开销。
2. 引入LENS框架：轻量级可观测性框架，提供专家专业化的可解释诊断，并跟踪持续重训练过程中的功能异构性。
3. 采用DL-AUC指标：结合全局排序性能与跨细分领域排序正确性的异构感知评估指标。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上的离线评估显示，该方法相比基线模型有持续提升；在线A/B测试证实了在信息流活跃度和探索指标上的增益。

Conclusion: 离线和在线结果共同验证了该方法是管理工业级推荐系统中异构性的有效手段，为构建统一且高性能的异构推荐排序模型提供了可行的工业实践案例。

Abstract: Heterogeneous recommendation feeds present complex challenges that extend beyond those found in highly homogeneous environments (e.g., music-only or video-only closed-ecosystem platforms). In Google Discover, a unified feed integrates diverse content sourced from the decentralized open web, including web articles, long-form and short-form videos, user-generated content (UGC), and beyond. Different content types exhibit distinct feature densities and user interaction patterns. Building a unified ranking model that sustains high performance across such heterogeneity, while avoiding negative transfer or majority bias, remains a significant industrial challenge.
  This paper presents an end-to-end case study on the industrial-scale multi-task ranking of heterogeneous feeds, grounded in real-world deployment. We introduce HA-MoE, a heterogeneity-adaptive multi-gated mixture-of-experts architecture that incorporates explicit heterogeneity context into both gating networks and expert representations. This approach enables effective specialization without significantly increasing operational overhead. To support reliable deployment, we introduce LENS, a lightweight observability framework that provides interpretable diagnostics of expert specialization and tracks this functional heterogeneity across continuous retraining. We evaluate our method using Dual-Level AUC (DL-AUC), a heterogeneity-aware evaluation metric that combines global ranking performance with cross-segment ranking correctness. Offline evaluations on a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate consistent improvements over baseline models. Furthermore, online A/B testing confirms gains in feed activity and exploration metrics. Together, offline and online results validate the effectiveness of our approach for managing heterogeneity in industrial-scale recommender systems.

</details>


### [178] [An Exploration Graph with Continuous Refinement for Efficient Multimedia Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27623)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“连续精炼探索图”(crEG)的新方法，用于快速构建紧凑的探索图，以解决大规模多媒体数据库中近似最近邻搜索(ANNS)面临的构建速度慢或内存占用高的问题。该方法特别针对“探索性搜索”场景进行了优化，并提供了可选的边缘优化算法以提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据集和特征向量维度的增长，大型多媒体数据库中的近似最近邻搜索(ANNS)变得日益重要。虽然基于图的方法在检索精度和搜索时间之间提供了最佳平衡，但现有方法存在构建速度慢或内存需求高的缺点。此外，现有的ANNS研究很少考虑查询本身也是数据库元素之一的“探索性搜索”场景，而这对于推荐和探索系统至关重要。

Method: 提出了“连续精炼探索图”(crEG)方法，这是一种新颖的快速构建紧凑探索图的策略。该算法专门设计用于生成和操作具有偶数度且保证随时连通性的无向图。此外，还提供了一种可选的边缘优化算法以进一步增强其有效性。

Result: 实验表明，crEG能够在保持最先进搜索性能的同时实现高效构建。研究还发现，在标准ANNS中表现高效率的方法并不一定能在“探索性搜索”中取得良好性能，突显了针对特定场景优化图结构的重要性。

Conclusion: crEG方法通过确保图的连通性和处理偶数度无向图，有效解决了传统ANNS方法在构建效率和内存方面的瓶颈，并为探索性搜索这一常被忽视但关键的场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As datasets and the dimensionality of feature vectors continue to grow, Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) in large multimedia databases becomes increasingly relevant. Graph-based approaches have demonstrated to offer the best trade-off between retrieval precision and search time. Despite their ability to deliver search times several orders of magnitude faster than exact search techniques, existing methods suffer from slow constructions speeds or high memory requirements. This paper presents a "continuous refining Exploration Graph" (crEG), a novel approach for rapidly constructing a compact exploration graph with state-of-the-art search performance. Additionally, it provides the ability to enhance its effectiveness even further through an optional edge optimization algorithm. Both algorithms are specifically designed to produce and operate on undirected graphs with even degrees and guarantee graph connectivity at any time, a property particularly valuable for "exploratory search", where the query is part of the database elements. Although such queries provide an advantageous starting point for graph search algorithms, they have been rarely considered in the context of ANNS, yet are crucial for recommendation and exploration systems. Our experiments demonstrate high efficiency in ANNS does not necessarily translate to a good performance in "exploratory search".

</details>


### [179] [Dynamic Exploration Graph: A Novel Approach for Efficient Nearest Neighbor Search in Evolving Multimedia Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27640)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Bruno Schilling,Konstantin Schall,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出动态探索图（DEG），一种支持动态数据的高效近似最近邻搜索算法，通过创新的顶点删除和图扩展机制，在保持静态数据集高性能的同时，显著提升动态场景下的构建时间和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有基于图的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）算法在处理动态数据集（数据持续增加或移除）时面临的挑战，同时兼顾搜索精度、时间效率及动态适应性。

Method: 提出动态探索图（DEG），其核心创新包括：1. 保证图连通性的新颖顶点删除算法；2. 与数据分布无关的图扩展方法。该方法作为连续精炼探索图的扩展，旨在维持平衡且高度连接的图结构。

Result: 在流式和在线场景下的实证实验表明，DEG在构建时间和搜索效率方面优于现有的动态图算法。此外，对于静态数据集，DEG的性能与当前最先进的方法相当，显示出广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: DEG成功解决了动态数据环境下的ANNS难题，在保证高搜索效率和低构建时间的同时，保持了与静态最优算法相当的精度，具有显著的应用价值。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) represents a fundamental problem in various applications (image-search, recommendation systems). While graph-based algorithms have demonstrated a good balance between search accuracy and time, handling dynamic datasets, where data points are continuously added or removed, remains a challenge. This paper introduces the Dynamic Exploration Graph (DEG), an extension of the continuous refining Exploration Graph, which retains high search efficiency for static dataset while adding essential support for dynamic data. At the core of the DEG design are two key innovations: a novel vertex deletion algorithm which guarantees graph connectivity and a data distribution-agnostic method for graph expansion. Through these mechanisms, the DEG maintains a balanced and well-connected structure, even under continuous data alterations. Empirical experiments in both streaming and online scenarios demonstrate the superior performance of the DEG, surpassing existing dynamic graph algorithms in terms of construction time and search efficiency. Although optimized for dynamic datasets, the DEG delivers results as good as current state-of-the-art approaches for static dataset, underscoring its broad applicability.

</details>


### [180] [LoopMemGR: From Behavior Logs to Evolving Memory for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27647)
*Hui Qian,Changfa Wu,Chang Liu,Binbin Cao,Jian Wu,Yuliang Yan,Han Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LoopMemGR框架，通过维护推荐经验日志（记录历史推荐-反馈轨迹），结合近期、频率和全局三种视图提取经验信号并压缩为经验Token，以解决生成式推荐中系统记忆不对称及无法复用历史决策反馈的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法遵循“历史即上下文”范式，仅从用户行为历史重构偏好，丢弃了系统的推荐决策及后续反馈。这导致系统记忆不对称：只记得用户做了什么，不记得系统推荐过什么或学到了什么，导致偏好验证信号、负面证据和历史探索信息无法跨请求复用。

Method: 提出LoopMemGR闭环推荐经验记忆框架。除传统行为日志外，维护推荐经验日志记录过去的推荐-反馈轨迹。通过三个互补视图提取与请求相关的证据：近期视图捕捉短期交互动态，频率视图总结重复的推荐模式，全局视图提炼跨用户的可迁移规律。将这些信号压缩为固定数量的经验Token，用于在有限输入预算下条件化生成骨干网络。

Result: 在工业级淘宝数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架能有效利用闭环经验积累和多视图经验提取，提升推荐效果。

Conclusion: 引入推荐经验记忆机制，通过多视图提取和压缩历史推荐反馈信息，解决了生成式推荐中系统侧记忆缺失的问题，实现了更有效的偏好验证和经验复用。

Abstract: Generative recommendation formulates next-item prediction as conditional autoregressive generation over discrete Semantic IDs, enabling end-to-end recommendation over large-scale item spaces. However, most existing methods follow a history-as-context paradigm that repeatedly reconstructs user preference from behavior history while discarding system-side recommendation decisions after each request. This creates an asymmetric memory: the system remembers what the user has done, but not what it has previously recommended or learned from the resulting feedback. Consequently, useful preference-validation signals, potential negative evidence, and historical exploration information cannot be directly reused across requests. To address these limitations, we propose LoopMemGR, a closed-loop recommendation experience memory framework for generative recommendation. In addition to the conventional behavior log, LoopMemGR maintains a recommendation experience log that records past recommendation--feedback trajectories. It extracts request-relevant evidence through three complementary views: the recency view captures short-term interaction dynamics, the frequency view summarizes recurring recommendation patterns, and the global view distills transferable regularities shared across users. These signals are compressed into a fixed number of experience tokens to condition the generative backbone under a bounded input budget. Extensive experiments on an industrial Taobao dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of closed-loop experience accumulation and multi-view experience extraction.

</details>


### [181] [Restoring Collaborative Signals in Semantic-ID Generative Recommendation via Personalized Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27682)
*Changjiang Han,Qingyang Li,Yaqiang Zang,Jikun Kang,Pinghua Gong,Xue Liu,Bowei He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于个性化自然语言的框架，通过添加分层协同线索来增强LLM生成式推荐模型的准确性，解决了语义ID（SID）无法同时容纳内容信息和协同信号的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的生成式推荐模型在将推荐任务转化为自回归生成项目语义ID（SID）时存在缺陷：文本和SID令牌嵌入空间未对齐，导致深度推理难以转化为正确的SID；且紧凑的SID无法同时承载内容信号和协同信号，两者相互竞争导致协同信号丢失，限制了推荐准确率。

Method: 提出了一种由个性化自然语言引导的框架。该方法不直接修改骨干网络或重新训练SID，而是在生成过程中引入分层协同线索。利用自然语言建立可分析的链接，将协同模式与其受众关联起来，从而恢复SID所缺失的协同信号。

Result: 在推理阶段通过将生成过程建立在协同结构基础上，实现了推荐准确率的持续稳定提升，无需依赖显式推理或多轮重训练。

Conclusion: 通过自然语言引入可靠的协同信号通道，可以有效解决SID表示能力不足的问题，显著提升LLM生成式推荐模型的性能。

Abstract: Making LLM-based generative recommendation models stronger and more personalized through natural language and explicit reasoning is a widely anticipated yet still unsolved goal. Such models cast recommendation as autoregressively generating an item's semantic-ID (SID), a short tuple of discrete codes, so that recommending well reduces to emitting the right SID. In this setting the model verbalizes its knowledge poorly, and text and SID tokens live in misaligned embedding spaces. Deep reasoning therefore rarely turns into a correct SID, and enabling explicit "thinking" often gives no gain or even hurts. The deeper cause is that a compact SID cannot hold content and collaborative signal at once: the two compete, and collaboration loses. Because a mis-predicted SID is a wrong recommendation, this caps accuracy directly. Costly multi-round training barely helps, and few methods try to supply the missing signal at inference time. What is missing is a reliable channel that carries collaborative signal into SID generation. We therefore propose a framework, guided by personalized natural language, that adds hierarchical collaborative cues as the model generates, without altering the backbone or retraining the SIDs. Rather than mapping language onto SIDs directly, it uses language to attach analyzable links between collaborative patterns and their audiences, restoring the collaborative signal that SIDs miss. The result is consistent gains in recommendation accuracy, grounding generation in collaborative structure at inference time rather than relying on explicit reasoning or retraining.

</details>


### [182] [Measuring Alignment With Reader Highlights Net of Position and Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27739)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估语言模型上下文压缩性能的方法，利用“自然社交高亮”作为非循环参考标准。通过消除位置和前缀长度偏差，研究发现基于语言模型的重要性排序在保留人类标记内容方面显著优于传统截断方法和经典启发式方法，其表现接近单个人类读者，且与GPT-5.4相当但低于Claude Opus 5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的上下文压缩评估依赖下游任务准确率，导致评估具有循环性（由另一个模型判断重要性）。现有的直接指标（如保留 crowd-marked 句子的比例）存在严重混淆：人群标记倾向于集中在文档开头且句子较长，导致任何偏好早期或长句子的方法都能获得高分，无法真实反映模型捕捉关键信息的能力。

Method: 1. **去偏匹配**：将每个被标记的句子与同一文档中未被标记的句子进行匹配，控制条件为“相对深度相等”和“文档内长度排名相等”，以消除位置和前缀长度的影响。
2. **合成零假设校准**：构建仅基于位置和长度的合成零假设数据来校准估计器，防止因分层不足导致的假阳性（研究发现仅按深度分层在20-36%的无效应零假设中会产生假阳性）。
3. **实验设置**：使用120个网页文档，每个文档至少有12位独立读者进行标记。对比语言模型重要性排序、朴素截断（基于位置）、以及经典方法（如Luhn启发式）。
4. **统计检验**：使用精确随机化检验计算p值，不假设聚类结构。

Result: 1. **语言模型优势**：语言模型重要性排序保留了38.4%的人群标记句子，而匹配的未标记邻居仅保留19.9%， enrichment 值为+0.196 [95% CI: +0.148, +0.239]，p=0.0005，结果跨厂商复现。
2. **基线对比**：朴素截断方法效果接近随机（+0.003）。经典方法Luhn (1958) 达到+0.088，表明仅靠词频计数可部分恢复读者选择能力，但加入词汇中心性后提升有限（+0.010），说明一致性并非主要来自中心性。
3. **人类 vs AI**：在相同预算和标签下，单个人类读者的表现（+0.182）与GPT-5.4 (+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]) 无显著差异，但低于Claude Opus 5。
4. **其他发现**：作者指出其先前工作中的某项主张在该语料库上未能复现。

Conclusion: 通过引入经过严格去偏和校准的自然社交高亮评估框架，研究证实了现代语言模型在识别对人类读者重要的文本片段方面具有显著能力，其表现可与单个人类读者媲美，甚至超越某些经典算法。该评估方法消除了传统指标的混淆因素，为上下文压缩技术提供了更可靠、非循环的基准。同时，研究也揭示了不同大模型之间在信息筛选能力上的差异（如Claude Opus 5优于GPT-5.4）。

Abstract: Context compression discards most of a document before a language model reads it, and is normally evaluated by downstream task accuracy - which makes another model the judge of what mattered. Naturalistic social highlighting offers a non-circular reference: many people independently marking passages on the same page. But the obvious metric, the fraction of crowd-marked sentences a compressor keeps, is confounded twice: crowd marks are front-loaded and crowd-marked sentences are longer, so any method favouring early or long sentences scores well regardless of readers. We remove both by matching each marked sentence against unmarked sentences of the same document at equal relative depth and equal within-document length rank, and we calibrate every estimator on synthetic nulls built from position and length alone - a step that matters, since depth-only stratification returns a false positive on 20-36% of nulls containing no effect. On 120 web documents (at least 12 independent readers each), a language-model importance ranking keeps 38.4% of crowd-marked sentences against 19.9% of their matched neighbours: an enrichment of +0.196 [+0.148, +0.239], at p = 0.0005 under an exact randomization test that assumes nothing about clustering, and replicated cross-vendor. Naive truncation, whose keep rule is position, correctly falls to +0.003. To give the number a scale: scored identically, on the same budget, against a crowd label recomputed to exclude them, a single human reader reaches +0.182 - indistinguishable from GPT-5.4 (+0.002 [-0.081, +0.088]) and below Claude Opus 5. Classical methods are not null - Luhn's 1958 heuristic reaches +0.088 - so reader selection is partly recoverable by counting words; conditioning additionally on lexical centrality removes only 0.010, so the agreement is not centrality. We also report that a claim in our own prior work does not reproduce on this corpus.

</details>


### [183] [A Structured Knowledge Infrastructure for Domain-Specific Data Asset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27748)
*Mengdi Chen,Yuanxin Huang,Yulin Jiang,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对企业数据分析代理中RAG检索错误率高（19.1%）及缺乏使用知识的问题，小红书提出了一种基于双层架构的解决方案。通过构建三层双重用途知识库、图引导检索器（GGR）和场景感知排序器（SAR），在保持低延迟的同时将Hit@10提升至96.6%，并显著减少Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决企业数据分析代理面临的两个结构性失败：通用RAG检索到错误资产（Hit@10仅为19.1%）以及无法提供防止指标误读的使用知识。这些问题源于语义鸿沟、实体歧义、模式漂移和资产-使用差距四大根本原因。

Method: 在小红书商业广告数据仓库（5300+ Hive表，14个领域）部署两层解决方案：1. 建立三层双重用途知识库（179份文档，八段注释模板），支持检索与生成，并通过闭环刷新管道实现日级更新（30秒热重载）；2. 采用图引导检索器（GGR），利用2859节点的知识图谱作为候选门控进行意图路由，减少71.6倍Token消耗；3. 应用场景感知排序器（SAR），结合19类实体识别和显式场景标注，利用负向知识提升检索精度。

Result: 在两个100题基准测试中，Hit@10从19.1%提升至96.6%（提升77.5个百分点），知识覆盖率从56%提升至77%。端到端延迟保持在4.84--5.33秒之间，其中负向知识单独贡献了25个百分点的Hit@10增益。

Conclusion: 该双层解决方案有效解决了企业级数据检索中的准确性和知识缺失问题，通过结构化知识管理和精细化检索排序机制，实现了高精度的数据资产定位和低延迟响应，为大规模企业数据仓库的智能分析提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Enterprise data analytics agents face two structural failures: generic RAG retrieves the wrong asset (Hit@10=19.1%) and delivers no usage knowledge to prevent metric misinterpretation---stemming from four root causes (C1--C4) ranging from semantic gap and entity ambiguity to schema drift and asset-usage gap. We present a two-layer solution deployed in the commercial advertising data warehouse at Xiaohongshu (5,300+ Hive tables, 14 domains). A three-tier dual-purpose knowledge base (179 documents, eight-section annotation template) serves both retrieval and generation, with a closed-loop refresh pipeline maintaining day-level freshness (one yes/no approval, 30s hot-reload). The Graph-Guided Retriever (GGR) uses a 2,859-node knowledge graph as a candidate gate with intent routing to deliver 71.6x token reduction. The Scene-Aware Ranker (SAR) applies 19-class entity recognition and explicit scenario annotations; negative knowledge alone contributes 25 percentage points of Hit@10 gain. On two 100-question benchmarks, Hit@10 rises from 19.1% to 96.6% (+77.5pp) and knowledge coverage from 56% to 77%, at 4.84--5.33s end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [184] [Hierarchical Latent Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27760)
*Peiyu Hu,Siying Gu,Weihai Lu,Zhuodong Liu,Yuntian Tang,Jiahao Liang,Yiying Xie,Jiang Rong,Zhaokai Luo,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HiLaR框架，通过分层潜在推理和层感知强化优化，解决LLM推荐中显式推理开销大及现有潜在推理方法贡献不明确的问题，在Amazon数据集上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐系统中，显式自然语言推理导致高计算开销，而现有的潜在推理方法主要关注中间状态的生成或验证，缺乏对逐层偏好角色及其贡献的充分刻画。

Method: 提出分层潜在推理（Hierarchical Latent Reasoning, HiLaR）框架：1) 构建时间引导的分层用户偏好表示；2) 将其与多个LLM潜在推理状态对齐，组织从广泛偏好到细粒度当前意图的推理过程；3) 结合最终推荐反馈与各状态边际目标似然增益得出的层感知过程奖励进行强化优化。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上的实验表明，HiLaR普遍优于强大的序列、生成式和基于LLM的推荐基线模型。消融实验和敏感性分析验证了分层表示学习、潜在对齐和过程级优化的有效性。

Conclusion: HiLaR通过分层潜在推理和过程奖励优化，有效提升了LLM在推荐任务中的性能，为理解潜在推理的逐层贡献提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for recommendation by leveraging their semantic understanding and contextual modeling capabilities. Recent studies further introduce reasoning mechanisms to improve user preference modeling. However, explicit natural-language reasoning incurs substantial inference overhead, whereas existing latent reasoning methods mainly focus on generating or verifying intermediate states, leaving their layer-wise preference roles and contributions insufficiently characterized. We propose HiLaR, a Hierarchical Latent Reasoning framework with layer-aware reinforcement optimization for LLM-based recommendation. HiLaR constructs temporal-guided hierarchical user preference representations, aligns them with multiple LLM latent reasoning states, and organizes the reasoning process from broad preferences to fine-grained current intents. To further optimize the reasoning trajectory, HiLaR combines final recommendation feedback with layer-aware process rewards derived from the marginal target-likelihood gain of each state. Experiments on four Amazon benchmark datasets show that HiLaR generally outperforms strong sequential, generative, and LLM-based recommendation baselines. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further verify the contribution of hierarchical representation learning, latent alignment, and process-level optimization. Our code is available in https://github.com/hupeiyu21/HiLaR.

</details>


### [185] [From Understanding to Action: Feedback-Grounded Policy Discovery for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27789)
*Zhi Chen,Minmao Wang,Xingchen Liu,Haoqiang Liang,Huihuang Lin,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Yulong Wang,Shijie Yi,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种反馈驱动的代理框架，通过区分意图知识与策略知识来解决大语言模型（LLM）在推荐系统中的“理解-行动差距”。该框架利用结果反馈发现并优化推荐策略，并通过双空间关系蒸馏将知识迁移到轻量级语义ID生成器中，实现了无需LLM的高效在线推理，并在A/B测试中显著提升了收益和广告价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语义ID的生成式推荐器主要依赖行为共现和局部转移信息，缺乏对用户当前需求的深层理解；而大语言模型（LLM）虽能推理异构交互历史，但其语言上的合理性并不等同于有效的推荐决策，存在“理解-行动差距”（Understanding-Action Gap）。

Method: 1. 区分意图知识（捕捉用户当前需求）与策略知识（指定推荐方向和拒绝边界）。2. 构建反馈驱动代理框架：首先诱导任务导向的意图，然后根据相对于仅意图基线的增量效用发现推荐策略。3. 使用结果衍生反馈而非语言合理性来评估和优化候选策略。4. 通过双空间关系蒸馏，将意图和策略知识迁移至轻量级语义ID生成器的两个潜在标记中。

Result: 在公开基准测试中，该方法持续优于基线模型；大规模在线A/B测试显示，收益（Revenue）提升4.506%，广告价值（ADVV）提升4.621%。

Conclusion: 通过弥合理解与行动之间的差距，并结合离线LLM推理与在线轻量级生成的优势，所提出的框架能有效提升推荐系统的性能，同时保持高效的在线推理能力。

Abstract: Semantic-ID-based generative recommenders enable efficient next-item generation, but their item-level supervision mainly captures behavioral co-occurrence and local transitions. Large language models (LLMs) can complement these models by reasoning over heterogeneous interaction histories to understand the user's current demand. However, LLMs are not inherently trained with recommendation-specific outcome feedback, and linguistically plausible reasoning therefore does not necessarily lead to effective recommendation decisions. We term this mismatch the Understanding-Action Gap. Accordingly, we distinguish intent knowledge, which captures the user's current demand, from policy knowledge, which specifies the recommendation direction and rejection boundary under that demand. To bridge this gap, we propose a feedback-driven agent framework that first induces task-oriented intent and then discovers recommendation policies according to their incremental utility over an intent-only baseline. Candidate policies are evaluated and refined using outcome-derived feedback rather than linguistic plausibility. We further transfer the resulting intent and policy knowledge into two latent tokens of a lightweight Semantic-ID generator through dual-space relational distillation, enabling LLM-free online inference. Experiments on public benchmarks show consistent improvements over baselines, while large-scale online A/B tests achieve gains of 4.506% in Revenue and 4.621% in ADVV.

</details>


### [186] [Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27944)
*Long Zhang,Hao Jiang,Sheng Yu,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LGRID框架，通过LLM驱动的生成式解耦方法解决现有SID推荐中语义纠缠和缺乏可解释性的问题，实现具有明确属性对应关系的可解释SID，在Kuaishou和Foursquare数据集上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义ID（SID）的推荐系统遵循“单表示后量化”范式，存在两大瓶颈：1. 语义纠缠导致地理、品牌、类别等异构属性混合，造成量化信息丢失和低质量SID碰撞；2. 黑盒表征学习缺乏显式的属性语义和清晰的SID位置含义，削弱了检索可靠性和对SID生成的诊断/控制能力。

Method: 提出LGRID（Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation），采用“编码->解耦->对齐->量化”流水线：1. 联合LLM编码保留跨属性的地理-语义依赖；2. 结构化解耦块将隐藏状态路由至地理和语义因子对应的属性对齐槽位；3. 协同对齐学习使槽位兼具生成解码性和检索判别性；4. 双流残差量化分别离散化两个流为具有显式属性对应关系的紧凑SID。

Result: 在Kuaishou和Foursquare数据集上，LGRID持续优于强SID基线，AUC相对提升最高达5.44%；粗粒度地理字段属性解码准确率超99%；全SID碰撞率降至39.9%（对比LGSID的97.0%）。

Conclusion: LGRID通过生成式解耦范式成功构建了可解释的SID，其位置扎根于物品属性和本地服务语义，有效解决了语义纠缠和信息丢失问题，提升了检索性能和SID可控性。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced ID-based recommendation through Semantic ID (SID) modeling, existing SID generation frameworks largely follow a single-representation-then-quantization paradigm. This design faces two bottlenecks: semantic entanglement mixes heterogeneous attributes, such as geography, brand, and category, causing information loss during quantization, low-quality SIDs, and severe collisions; moreover, black-box representation learning provides neither explicit attribute semantics nor clear geographic or semantic meanings for SID positions. These limitations weaken both retrieval reliability and the ability to diagnose or control SID generation. We propose Interpretable Representation via LLM-Driven Generative Disentanglement for Local-Life Service Recommendation (LGRID). LGRID introduces a generative disentanglement paradigm through an Encode -> Disentangle -> Align -> Quantize pipeline. It first uses joint LLM encoding to preserve cross-attribute geographic-semantic dependencies, rather than encoding fields independently. A Structured Disentangled Block then routes hidden states into attribute-aligned slots for geographic and semantic factors. Synergistic Alignment Learning makes these slots both generatively decodable and discriminative for retrieval, while Dual-Stream Residual Quantization separately discretizes the two streams into compact SIDs with explicit attribute correspondence. This design yields interpretable SIDs with positions grounded in item attributes and local-service semantics. Experiments on Kuaishou and Foursquare show that LGRID consistently outperforms strong SID baselines, achieving up to a 5.44 percent relative AUC gain. It also achieves over 99 percent attribute-decoding accuracy for coarse geographic fields and reduces the full-SID collision rate to 39.9 percent, compared with 97.0 percent for LGSID.

</details>


### [187] [VIG-RL: Learning to Search and Insert for Verified Image Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28055)
*Qinhan Yu,Jun Guang,Chong Chen,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出VIG-RL框架，通过强化学习和ReAct循环解决知识密集型场景中动态图像定位与插入问题，超越现有静态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强框架依赖解耦、静态管道，无法动态推理何时需要外部知识及如何上下文插入视觉资产，导致在需要可靠图文交错响应的场景中表现不足。

Method: 提出VIG-RL自主智能体框架，将搜索-选择-插入工作流构建为主动决策过程；采用动态ReAct风格循环，并通过包含逐步工具执行评估和最终多模态对齐的复合奖励系统进行强化学习优化。

Result: 广泛评估表明，VIG-RL建立了新的最先进水平（SOTA），显著优于现有的静态基线方法。

Conclusion: VIG-RL通过动态决策机制有效解决了Verified Image Grounding (VIG) 中的关键挑战，证明了强化学习在动态多模态生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: In knowledge-intensive scenarios, providing reliable interleaved text-image responses requires Verified Image Grounding (VIG): the precise integration of retrieved authentic visual evidence. Existing retrieval-augmented frameworks predominantly rely on decoupled, static pipelines, inherently failing to dynamically reason about when external knowledge is required and where visual assets should be contextually inserted. To bridge this gap, we propose VIG-RL, an autonomous agentic framework that formulates the search-selection-insertion workflow as an active decision-making process. Operating within a dynamic ReAct-style loop, VIG-RL is optimized via reinforcement learning, guided by a composite reward system that holistically evaluates the agent's step-by-step tool execution and final multimodal alignment. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that VIG-RL establishes a new state-of-the-art, significantly outperforming existing static baselines.

</details>


### [188] [CCFormer: Efficient Cross-Field Interaction and Hierarchical Sequence Compression for Industrial Recommendation at Tencent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28070)
*Yunlong Wang,Huizhe Zhang,Haonan Hu,Yudong Li,Bing Wen,Jianchao Tu,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CCFormer，一种高效的Transformer骨干网络，旨在解决工业推荐系统中计算开销与细粒度特征交互之间的平衡难题。通过结合跨字段特征分离交叉注意力和长序列子空间token混合，实现了异构特征域间的长期偏好信号利用及高效长序列建模。在公开基准和腾讯大规模工业数据集上表现优异，并在视频推荐和广告排序场景中在线A/B测试中显著提升CTR和广告收入，同时加速训练2.21倍，已全量部署于腾讯生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于自注意力的顺序推荐模型在增加序列长度和模型容量时展现出可预测的扩展规律，但实际推荐系统面临严格的延迟和资源限制。现有的方法难以在计算开销与细粒度特征交互之间取得良好平衡，特别是在处理长序列和异构特征域时存在挑战。

Method: 提出CCFormer，其核心包括：1）特征-字段分离的交叉注意力机制，用于跨异构特征域进行特征交互；2）长序列子空间token混合，以挖掘长期偏好信号；3）层级序列压缩策略，具有逐渐扩大的感受野，从而在减少信息损失的前提下实现高效的长序列建模。

Result: 1）在两个公共基准数据集和一个大规模工业数据集上的实验表明，CCFormer consistently优于最先进（SOTA）的基线模型；2）在腾讯的视频推荐场景中获得3.57%的点击率（CTR）提升，在广告排序场景中获得1.71%的广告收入增长；3）相比强基线HSTU，模型训练速度加速2.21倍。

Conclusion: CCFormer成功统一了跨字段特征交互和压缩长序列建模，证明了其在工业推荐系统中的实用性和有效性。该模型已在腾讯的生产推荐系统中全量部署，服务于主要流量，验证了其在严格资源约束下的高性能和高效率。

Abstract: Recent studies in industrial recommendation systems have demonstrated that sequential recommendation models built upon self-attention can benefit from predictable scaling laws by increasing sequence length and model capacity. However, practical recommender systems impose strict latency and resource constraints, making it challenging to balance computational overhead with fine-grained feature interaction. In this paper, we propose CCFormer, an efficient Transformer backbone that unifies cross-field feature interaction and compressed long-sequence modeling for industrial recommendation. Specifically, CCFormer combines feature-field separated cross attention with long-sequence subspace token mixing to exploit long-term preference signals across heterogeneous feature domains. A hierarchical sequence compression strategy with progressively expanded receptive fields enables efficient long-sequence modeling with reduced information loss. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset demonstrate that CCFormer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Online A/B tests in a video recommendation scenario and an advertising ranking scenario at Tencent further validate its industrial practicality, yielding a 3.57% CTR gain and a 1.71% advertising revenue lift, respectively, while accelerating model training by 2.21x over the strong HSTU baseline. CCFormer has been fully deployed in Tencent's production recommendation system, serving the main traffic of both scenarios.

</details>


### [189] [TCA-SIR: Learning Target-Conditioned Abstractions for Scientific Inspiration Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28498)
*Yuto Suzuki,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TCA-SIR方法，将科学灵感检索重构为目标条件抽象，通过生成针对目标的可迁移抽象原则来提升远程灵感的检索效果，在ResearchBench上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学灵感检索（SIR）方法仅基于主题相似性排序论文，无法显式表达候选灵感如何转移到目标问题，限制了远程灵感（其价值在于可复用的解题原理而非主题重叠）的发现。受人类从源领域抽象可迁移方面并重新映射到新目标过程的启发，旨在改进SIR以更好地捕捉这种转移能力。

Method: 将SIR重构为目标条件抽象（Target-Conditioned Abstraction, TCA）。检索对象是从候选文献中提取的、专门为目标问题定制的“可迁移抽象原则”。提出TCA-SIR模型，学习生成这些目标条件的抽象表示，并利用其预测转移性。

Result: 在ResearchBench数据集上，TCA-SIR优于先前的SIR方法和直接的大语言模型（LLM）检索。与MOOSE-Chem相比，HitRate@top4提升了超过10个百分点。学习到的抽象比未训练的TCA提示词更清晰地恢复了与目标相关的机制，提供了更强的检索能力和可解释的推理依据。

Conclusion: 通过将科学灵感检索转化为目标条件抽象任务，TCA-SIR能有效识别和利用远程灵感中的可复用原理，不仅提高了检索准确率，还增强了科学发现过程的可解释性。

Abstract: Scientific hypothesis generation for AI for Science typically involves Scientific Inspiration Retrieval (SIR) followed by hypothesis composition. Existing SIR methods rank papers by topical similarity and do not explicitly represent how a candidate inspiration transfers to a target problem. This is especially limiting for remote inspirations, whose value often lies in reusable problem-solving principles rather than topical overlap. Motivated by how humans abstract transferable aspects of a source and remap them to a new target, we reformulate SIR as target-conditioned abstraction (TCA). The retrieval object is a transferable abstract principle extracted from a candidate specifically for the target. We present TCA-SIR, which learns to generate target-conditioned abstractions and uses their representations to predict transferability. On ResearchBench, TCA-SIR outperforms prior SIR methods and direct LLM retrieval, improving HitRate@top4% over MOOSE-Chem by more than 10 percentage points. Learned abstractions also recover target-relevant mechanisms more clearly than an untrained TCA prompt, yielding both stronger retrieval and an interpretable rationale for scientific inspiration.

</details>


### [190] [MagicSelector: Joint Optimization for Agent Tool Selection via Counterfactual Decomposition and Progressive Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17751)
*HONOR Agentic Search Team,Zhengzong Chen,Lei Tang,Lijun Liu,Chuandi Jiang,Fan Yang,Keyun Chu,Chu Zhao,Shihao Liu,Minghang Li,Bo Liang,Can Wen,Hailong Wu,Jingnan Ju,Mian Liu,Nengbin Zhang,Peiqiang Wang,Penghe Nie,Qinhui Gu,Sijia Lv,Siqi Chen,Wei Zhang,Yang Xu,Yuhao Qian,Yuxiang Zhang,Zeng Cheng,Zhen Wang,Zuan Chen,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MagicSelector框架，通过反事实任务分解、渐进式重排序和动态Top-K策略，解决智能体工具检索中的歧义指令转换及域外场景噪声问题，在MTDTool基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体工具检索中面临的根本挑战：将模糊的用户指令转化为可执行原子子任务，以及在域外（OOD）场景中缓解冗余噪声和严重的上下文干扰。

Method: 构建包含三大核心组件的联合优化框架：(1) 偏好引导的反事实任务分解机制，利用反事实奖励量化分解对检索排名的边际因果增益；(2) 基于自蒸馏难负样本挖掘的渐进式工具重排序方法，优化点级和列表级相关性；(3) 双语义边界感知的动态Top-K策略，自适应监控重排序分数悬崖和工具间语义变化以截断候选列表。

Result: 在构建的移动多轮交互任务分解基准MTDTool上进行评估，实验表明MagicSelector在工具检索准确率、OOD泛化能力以及整体Token效率方面均显著优于最先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 提出的联合优化框架能有效提升工具检索的精度与鲁棒性，特别是在处理复杂指令和域外场景时具有显著优势，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: We present MagicSelector, a joint optimization framework integrating Counterfactual task decomposition, Progressive reranking, and Dynamic Top-K, designed to address the fundamental challenges of tool retrieval in agents. MagicSelector is a specialized framework capable of translating ambiguous user instructions into executable atomic subtasks and guiding high-precision tool retrieval, effectively mitigating redundant noise and severe context distraction in out-of-domain (OOD) scenarios. We empower MagicSelector with these capabilities through three key contributions: (1) a preference-guided counterfactual task decomposition mechanism that utilizes a counterfactual reward to quantify the marginal causal gain of decomposition on retrieval ranking, effectively imposing fine-grained structural supervision on logical coherence; (2) a progressive tool reranking method driven by self-distillation hard negative mining, which optimizes both point-wise and list-wise relevance to enhance fine-grained discrimination among highly similar tools; and (3) a dual semantic boundary-aware dynamic Top-K strategy that adaptively monitors reranking score cliffs and inter-tool semantic shifts to dynamically truncate the candidate list, maximizing relevant tool recall while filtering long-tail noise. Evaluated on MTDTool, the first task decomposition benchmark we constructed tailored for mobile multi-turn interactions with process-level annotations, MagicSelector yields promising performance. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MagicSelector significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of tool retrieval accuracy, OOD generalization capability, and overall token efficiency, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多头注意力残差（MHAR）机制，通过为每个特征子空间分配独立的深度历史路由查询，解决标准Transformer中单查询共享导致的子空间冲突问题。在100M至8B参数规模的模型上验证了其在降低验证损失和提升下游任务性能方面的有效性，且计算开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的注意力残差机制使用单一查询向量覆盖整个宽度，导致不同特征子空间必须通过同一分布读取深度历史。随着模型宽度增加，子空间间对“读取哪些层”的分歧加剧，造成性能瓶颈。

Method: 引入多头注意力残差（MHAR），将路由查询重塑为H个子空间头，每个头拥有独立的softmax分布以读取深度历史。该操作形成块对角线读取结构，不增加参数量且计算开销可忽略。同时开发Fused Triton路由内核以优化训练吞吐量，并利用delta注意力残差进行恒等映射转换以支持大规模中期训练。

Result: 在去重、质量过滤且侧重STEM和代码的Nemotron语料库上，MHAR在100M、350M和1B规模下均优于标准Transformer及其他四种方法，验证损失分别降低0.061、0.149和0.140。超参数H呈现U型曲线，最优值稳定在H=4或H=8，H=8被用于大模型。H=16时收益回落。8B模型中期训练后，GSM8K得分提升+3.2，GPQA提升+3.1。训练吞吐量提升至基线的0.55-0.88x，内存占用保持接近基线水平。

Conclusion: 子空间分歧是限制注意力残差性能的关键因素，MHAR通过多头路由有效解决了这一问题。该方法在不显著增加计算成本的前提下，显著提升了不同规模模型的预训练效果和下游推理能力，证明了多头路由作为设计轴的有效性。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [192] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KernelGenBench基准，系统评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核在多种算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现。研究发现：智能体方法优于纯LLM采样；跨平台性能下降严重；自主生成成本高昂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型增加了对高效加速器内核的需求，但内核开发仍专业且耗时。现有缺乏全面基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在不同算子源和异构硬件平台上的表现。

Method: 构建KernelGenBench统一基准，包含两个子基准：KernelGenBench-MS（多源，评估来自三个非PyTorch核心的210个算子）和KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片，使用110个算子子集在六款异构硬件平台上测量性能可移植性）。进行大规模评估，消耗超过150亿token。

Result: (1) 基于智能体的方法始终优于纯LLM采样方法，cuBLAS算子是所有方法中最具挑战性的；(2) 生成性能在不同硬件平台间差异显著，即使是专门的kernel智能体也经历严重的跨平台性能下降（例如AutoKernel从NVIDIA平台的87%降至Platform E的25%）；(3) 自主内核生成成本高昂，专用智能体方法平均每成功生成一个算子需5.11百万token（AKO4all为5.19百万），远高于简单LLM采样方法。

Conclusion: 当前LLM/智能体生成的内核在性能可移植性和生成效率方面仍存在重大挑战，特别是在跨硬件平台和复杂算子上，且计算资源消耗巨大。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [193] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源Python库，旨在解决放射学图像深度学习中的异构数据集成难题。它提供统一API加载、协调和增强数据，标准化24个公共数据集的元数据，支持多种标注类型，并引入AI代理简化新数据集集成流程。通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、格式、目录结构、标签模式和注释类型的异构放射学数据集，这一过程复杂且门槛高。

Method: 开发RadHarmony库：1) 将24个公共数据集的元数据标准化为单一表格格式；2) 封装MONAI map-style datasets以提供深度学习就绪的数据样本及可选磁盘缓存；3) 通过单一接口支持分类标签、分割掩码、边界框和报告文本；4) 提供交互式可视化工具；5) 引入AI-agent技能指导从原始数据检查到代码生成和测试的全集成工作流。

Result: 成功实现了异构数据的无缝集成，无需针对特定数据集编写专用代码即可预训练RadHarmony-ViT（视觉Transformer基线模型）。代码和预训练权重已公开。

Conclusion: RadHarmony显著降低了放射学数据集集成的技术壁垒，提高了多源数据利用效率，为医学影像分析提供了标准化的工具支持。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [194] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究指出标准模型合并方法在结合不同安全目标时存在显著偏差：拒绝攻击的能力转移远优于有害内容分类能力，导致合并后的模型倾向于广泛拒绝而非精准识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标准模型合并方法是否能同时保留多个与安全相关的行为（如有害内容分类和对抗性拒绝），以及合并过程中是否存在行为保真度的不对称性。

Method: 对Gemma-3-1B-IT模型进行两种互补安全目标的微调（CARES有害分类和WildJailbreak对抗拒绝），并采用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种方法进行模型合并，随后评估合并模型在分类准确率、攻击抵抗力和良性合规性上的表现，并通过权重空间测量分析任务向量的方向和幅度。

Result: 所有四种合并方法均显示攻击抵抗力显著优于分类准确率：合并模型保留了81-85%的越狱拒绝率，但CARES准确率降至最高仅12.9%。这种不对称性并非由于任务向量方向对立（余弦相似度仅为0.011），而是因为拒绝微调产生的每层任务向量幅度更大，导致对幅度敏感的方法偏向于应用拒绝更新。

Conclusion: 当与安全相关的任务向量在尺度上存在显著差异时，标准模型合并可能导致安全识别能力坍塌为广泛的拒绝行为，表明现有合并方法在处理规模不一的安全目标时存在局限性。

Abstract: Model merging is often used to combine capabilities from separately fine-tuned models without additional training, but it is unclear whether standard merging methods preserve multiple safety-relevant behaviors simultaneously. We study this question through a controlled case study using two Gemma-3-1B-IT finetunes on two complementary safety objectives: CARES harm-level classification and WildJailbreak adversarial refusal. We merge the two fine-tunes using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and DARE-TIES, and evaluate the merged models on classification accuracy, attack resistance, and benign compliance. Across all four methods, attack resistance transfers significantly more than classification accuracy: merged models retain 81-85% jailbreak refusal rates while CARES accuracy falls to at most 12.9%. Weight-space measurements suggest that this asymmetry is not caused by strongly opposing task-vector directions: the two task vectors are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.011). Instead, the refusal fine-tune induces consistently larger per-layer task-vector magnitudes, causing magnitude-sensitive methods to favor refusal updates. These results show that standard model merging can collapse safety recognition into broad refusal when safety-relevant task vectors differ substantially in scale.

</details>


### [195] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Divergence Decoding，一种无需训练的推理时能力融合框架。通过Jensen-Shannon散度监测通用模型与领域专家模型间的分布差异，动态路由控制以结合两者的逻辑推理与领域知识优势，在科学基准测试中超越单一模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型（通用模型）缺乏专业科学知识，而领域专用模型（专家模型）存在逻辑能力下降和鲁棒性降低这一矛盾困境。

Method: 将推测解码的“草稿-验证”骨架重构为自适应路由机制。核心是利用Jensen-Shannon散度监控两个模型在每个token上的分布分歧。当专家模型出现显著分歧时，识别为潜在推理风险，并即时将控制权路由给通用模型，实现推理时的动态策略组合。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama系列模型上，针对GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等挑战性科学基准进行评估。结果表明，该方法优于单独的领域专用模型和通用模型，有效超越了大多数单模型基线的性能。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用的、无需训练的方法论，通过推理时的自适应协作来融合多样化的LLM能力。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [196] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于描述逻辑的统一框架PIE，包含增量归约模块PIE-Abducer和规划模块PIE-APT，通过直接推导而非组合搜索解决动态知识图谱中的不完整信息推理与规划问题，在OWL基准测试中优于传统规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作形式化方法面临可判定性问题及Ramification Problem，且通过结构归约处理不完整知识需进行庞大的组合搜索，难以适应开放世界环境。

Method: 构建统一框架：1) PIE-Abducer：将目标否定注入一致分支，通过直接反驳后果提取缺失前提，避免最小击中集枚举；2) PIE-APT：采用递归生成-测试架构，结合回溯A*搜索与PIE-Abder（受限因果深度），并通过前向时间投影严格验证。所有操作原生于描述逻辑，无需外部模态算子。

Result: 在四个强调经典规划所缺乏语义能力的OWL基准测试中表现优异；定性上优于经典规划器；定量上证明其直接推导方法在归约丰富过程中优于基于最小击中集的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过原生支持描述逻辑和增量直接推导机制，有效解决了动态知识图谱中不完整信息的推理与规划难题，兼具逻辑可判定性与计算效率优势。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [197] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAUSE，一个面向用户的基准测试，用于评估个人AI助手在状态感知、服务集成环境中的表现，解决现有基准碎片化和忽略用户状态的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试往往割裂服务上下文或抽象掉用户状态，限制了其在真实服务环境中评估以用户为中心的个人助手行为的能力。现实场景中，助手需处理持久化用户状态、尊重特定配置和权限，并维持多服务间的长期约束交互。

Method: 引入PAUSE基准测试，包含显式用户-代理交互（通过逼真的用户模拟），采用多阶段评估框架：对开放式任务使用语义和轨迹级行为指标，对强约束任务使用确定性状态验证。同时提供用户中心化的合成流水线，以可扩展地生成连贯的服务环境、用户配置和标注任务。

Result: 最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置意识的场景下，任务完成率均低于70%，揭示了稳定且可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: PAUSE为评估个人AI助手在复杂、状态依赖的服务环境中的能力提供了原则性、可复现的框架，并展示了当前先进模型在此类任务上的显著不足，同时其合成管道支持基准的可扩展性和未来研究。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [198] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillMentor框架，通过强化学习训练诊断策略来识别智能体的“盲点”并生成纠正技能，从而在不更新执行器权重或依赖人工标注的情况下实现自我进化，在AppWorld和BFCLv3基准上平均提升44.2%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体自我进化研究主要关注如何行动（执行），而忽视了发现自身未知领域（即“盲点诊断”）这一同等重要的能力。现有方法通常假设失败发现是已知的，仅关注如何修复已识别的失败，且往往依赖执行器适应或人类监督。本研究旨在探索是否可以将“盲点诊断”本身作为一种可学习的能力，独立于执行过程进行优化。

Method: 提出了SkillMentor框架，其核心是通过强化学习训练一个Mentor（导师）策略。该策略负责生成诊断任务、识别反复出现的失败模式，并将这些模式整理为可重用的纠正技能（corrective skills）。实验设定排除了执行器适应和人类监督两种替代进步来源，确保性能提升完全源于学习到的诊断能力。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准测试中，SkillMentor使执行器的性能平均提升了44.2%。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断是一种可学习的能力。通过分离诊断与执行，智能体可以在不更新执行器权重且不依赖人工 curated 数据的情况下实现自我进化。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [199] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对比实验发现，扩散语言模型（DLMs）虽能抵抗基于梯度的对抗攻击，但对自然噪声缺乏固有防御，且存在过度自信问题。机制分析表明，模型能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性源于解码器路由失败而非架构缺陷，因此鲁棒性需从迭代解码循环层面进行根本性整合，而非表面修补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散语言模型（DLMs）提供了双向上下文和迭代优化的优势，但其在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性与校准特性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 使用参数匹配的配对设计（LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B 和 Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B），在32种自然扰动条件、对抗梯度探测及机制隐藏状态分析下，系统评估DLM相对于自回归（AR）基线的鲁棒性与校准表现。

Result: 1. DLM的高随机性损失景观可抵抗基于梯度的对抗后缀，但对自然噪声无保证防御，证明日常鲁棒性依赖权重而非架构；2. DLM表现出系统性过度自信，构成部署风险；3. 机制探测显示所有模型均完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全归因于解码器路由失败；4. 表面级提示修补无法改善噪声基线性能。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性无法通过表面补丁解决，必须将其根本性地整合到迭代解码循环中。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [200] [Bridging Inference-Time Scaling and Episodic Memory with Action-Centric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27415)
*Xu Zheng,Chaohao Lin,Zhuomin Chen,Weijieying Ren,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GAMER框架，通过基于图的行动中心记忆和双流TD学习，解决LLM代理推理时的状态无效和计算冗余问题，显著提升推理效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时间扩展方法在智能体应用中存在状态无效率和冗余搜索问题；现有记忆机制依赖LLM推理能力，导致高昂的计算成本。

Method: 构建动态“行动中心图”建模历史推理；采用双流时序差分（TD）学习机制评估动作节点的正向（建议）和负向（规避）价值；将记忆机制与LLM解耦以减少上下文token消耗。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，相比原始基线方法，成功率和进度率分别提升了20.81%和6.17%，显著提高了推理扩展的效率。

Conclusion: GAMER通过结合图结构记忆与强化学习价值估计，有效桥接了推理扩展与情景记忆，实现了更高效、低成本的复杂推理智能体系统。

Abstract: Recent advancements in inference-time scaling have significantly unlocked the complex reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models~(LLMs). However, for agents, these approaches suffer from a critical inefficiency, operating in a stateless manner and engaging in redundant search processes. Existing memory mechanisms largely rely on the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, leading to prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, \textit{GAMER}~(Graph-based Action-centric Memory with Episodic Reasoning), that bridges the gap between inference scaling and episodic memory. Our approach models historical reasoning as a dynamic \textit{Action-Centric Graph}. By decoupling the memory mechanism from LLMs, our method can save token/money usage by providing less memory context than memory mechanism baselines. To extract knowledge from the graph effectively, we use a dual-stream Temporal Difference learning mechanism to estimate the positive~(suggestion) and negative~(avoidance) value of action nodes based on past successes and failures. During the inference phase, this learned value function optimizes decision-making bi-directionally, so that positive values provide action suggestions, while negative values indicate high-risk actions. By performing efficient searches on the graph, our method significantly improves the efficiency of inference scaling. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \textit{GAMER} achieves superior performance by \textbf{20.81\%/6.17\%} for success/progress rate compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Dimensionality and Measurement Precision in HLE's Multiple-Choice Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27420)
*Mayank Sharma,Savira Nadela,Tyler Matteson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过心理测量学方法评估Humanity's Last Exam (HLE)，发现HLE实际仅测量单一通用推理因子，其领域子分数不具备独立能力解释力，且对顶尖模型的区分度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管HLE广泛用于评估前沿语言模型，并假设其八个领域的子分数代表不同的能力，但尚无研究验证这些标签是否对应经验上可分离的潜在构念，以及该基准是否能有效区分能力相似的模型。

Method: 对29个LLM在HLE文本多选子集（428题）上进行评估，采用项目反应理论（IRT）的双参数逻辑模型分析基准的维度结构和测量精度分布。

Result: 1. HLE测量的是单一通用推理因子（McDonald's ω_h = 0.998）；2. 领域标签仅解释3.5%的项目响应方差；3. 领域内/间残差相关性几乎相同（Cohen's d = 0.016）；4. 领域特定能力估计与总分高度冗余（r ≥ 0.81）；5. 测量精度集中在中等能力水平，而在θ > 0的前沿模型区域急剧下降。

Conclusion: HLE的领域子分数不支持对其能力的独特性解释，且该基准区分最强模型的能力受到限制。

Abstract: Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) is widely used to evaluate frontier language models. HLE organizes its questions into eight subject-domain categories, whose subscores are often interpreted as evidence of distinct capabilities. However, no study has assessed whether these labels correspond to empirically separable latent constructs, nor whether the benchmark effectively differentiates between models of similar ability. We evaluate 29 LLMs on the text-only multiple-choice subset of HLE ($J = 428$ items) and apply psychometric methods to assess both the dimensionality of the benchmark and the distribution of its measurement precision. Fitting a two-parameter logistic IRT model, we find convergent evidence that HLE measures a single general reasoning factor: McDonald's $ω_h = 0.998$, domain labels explain only 3.5\% of item response variance, within- and between-domain residual correlations are nearly identical (Cohen's $d = 0.016$), and domain-specific ability estimates are near-redundant with the total score ($r \geq 0.81$). A separate analysis of the test information function reveals that measurement precision concentrates at moderate ability levels and drops sharply above $θ= 0$, where frontier models sit. These findings suggest that HLE's domain subscores do not warrant distinct capability interpretations and that the benchmark's ability to discriminate among the strongest models is limited.

</details>


### [202] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“轨迹图副驾驶”（Trajectory Graph Copilot）的新框架，通过模拟软件调试中的执行日志分析，在动作执行前利用图神经网络诊断潜在错误，从而提升大语言模型智能体在长视界交互任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的智能体在复杂交互任务中表现优异，但在具身AI等领域的长视界任务中，由于动作空间巨大且复杂，单一次非最优动作会导致误差累积，致使智能体耗尽有限的步骤预算而无法完成任务。现有方法若通过昂贵的微调来解决此问题并不高效。

Method: 受软件调试启发，提出了“轨迹图副驾驶”框架。其核心是“图调试器”（Graph Debugger），它将历史轨迹建模为概率图，并利用图神经网络识别导致失败的序列动作模式。该机制作为主动诊断沙盒，在执行前提供潜在缺陷动作的预警，促使智能体自我纠正，无需进行昂贵的模型微调。

Result: 在四个基准测试集上对三种LLM智能体进行了广泛实验，结果显示平均通过率提升了14.69%。该方法有效防止了 costly mistakes（昂贵错误），显著增强了智能体完成长视界任务的能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于图神经网络的预行动错误诊断机制，Trajectory Graph Copilot 能够以低成本显著提升LLM智能体在长视界、高复杂度交互任务中的鲁棒性和成功率，为解决误差累积问题提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully. The extensive experiments on four benchmarks with three LLM agents demonstrate a $14.69\%$ pass ratio improvement on average.

</details>


### [203] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VAmoS Bench，一个端到端评估语音代理系统在客服任务中“隔离率”（Containment）表现的基准测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音代理基准测试多关注组件质量（如词错误率、延迟），缺乏对系统整体是否成功独立解决电话呼叫（即“隔离率”）的衡量。

Method: 构建包含100个场景的模拟环境，每个场景设定用户目标和后端数据库状态。代理Riley通过音频与模拟用户交互，并调用真实SQL工具执行操作。使用基于完整交互轨迹和二元断言的自动评分器评估结果，涵盖约三分之一的对抗性压力场景。

Result: 实现了对语音代理系统在特定金融客服任务中端到端性能的量化评估，能够检测代理是否在未更新数据库的情况下声称已处理，或在正确操作时泄露隐私信息。

Conclusion: VAmoS Bench填补了语音代理系统整体效能评估的空白，支持持续更新的排行榜，为未来扩展任务和场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: Production voice agents span cascaded, speech-to-speech, and hybrid architectures. Voice-agent benchmarks typically measure component quality and conversational properties such as word error rate, latency, naturalness, and turn-taking. Fewer measure whether the agent handled a phone call correctly on its own. Contact centers refer to this as ``containment'': the share of phone calls the automated system resolves without handing off to a human. On some phone calls the right outcome is refusal or a redirect. To address this gap, we introduce VAmoS Bench, the Voice Agent Simulation Bench. It measures complete voice-agent systems end to end in a stateful customer-support task. The agent is Riley, a credit-card support representative for a fictional bank who can freeze, cancel, replace, or activate a card. Each of 100 scenarios supplies a simulated caller with a private goal and a seeded PostgreSQL backend. The platform uses each scenario to populate and activate an isolated simulation in which the caller reaches Riley over audio; roughly one-third apply adversarial pressure. The agent can use five tools that execute real SQL against the backend. Each scenario also defines binary assertions. A grader evaluates them against the complete trace of what the caller and agent said and what the agent did, including tool invocations, arguments, and returned rows. This catches an agent that claims to have changed a card without updating the database, as well as one that makes the right database change while disclosing protected information. This first benchmark version focuses on financial services. Its evaluation protocol supports an evolving leaderboard: additional voice agents can be evaluated on the same version, while later versions can expand the tasks and scenarios.

</details>


### [204] [Skill Use or Skill Theater? Evaluating the Reasoning Backroom in Skill-Augmented Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27484)
*Jinwei Hu,Yi Qi,Xinmiao Huang,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了语言智能体中存在的“推理后台”（Reasoning Backroom）现象，即声明的技能使用与实际因果影响之间存在系统性差距。为此提出BACKTRACE评估框架和BACKROOMBench测试集，通过反事实干预证明传统的观察式检测无法准确识别技能对决策的真实贡献，确立了该问题为通用的AI溯源难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能可用性评估主要依赖智能体的自我归因或可见推理过程，这些信号仅反映表面行为而非技能是否真正改变了决策结果。为了验证技能增强型智能体是否存在声明使用与实际干预影响之间的系统性偏差，即“推理后台”现象，需要一种能够测量真实因果影响的评估方法。

Method: 提出了BACKTRACE评估框架，该方法为每个技能条件答案匹配无技能的反事实对照组，对技能的含义、措辞、身份、内容和分配进行干预，并在答案确定后才 elicitation 归因信息。基于此框架构建了BACKROOMBench，涵盖受控逻辑题、竞赛数学、多种技能条件、单/多智能体设置及多样化模型家族。

Result: 发现普遍的溯源失败现象：声明的技能使用往往保持稳定，但因果依赖性和效用却发生变化，导致“无声采纳”和“表演性使用”。行为效应更依赖于程序内容而非显示的技能身份；声明的归因对人工制品的可用性反应强烈。基于直接声称、文本提及、轨迹相似度和LLM裁判的观察式检测器均无法准确识别决策对技能的真实依赖。在多智能体系统中，即使来源丢失，技能影响仍可能通过通信存活，而无技能团队也会虚构技能和来源。

Conclusion: “推理后台”是一个通用的AI溯源问题，其审计必须依赖干预手段而非观察数据。传统基于声明或痕迹的检测方法不可靠，亟需引入反事实干预来评估技能对决策的实际因果影响。

Abstract: Reusable skills are becoming a standard interface for extending language agents with task procedures. Yet evaluators usually infer skill use from visible reasoning or the agent's own attribution. These signals show what the agent appears to use, not whether the skill changed its decision. We ask whether skill-augmented agents exhibit a \textbf{Reasoning Backroom}, a systematic gap between stated skill use and intervention-measured influence. We introduce BACKTRACE, an evaluation framework that pairs each skill-conditioned answer with a matched no-skill counterfactual, intervenes on skill meaning, wording, identity, content, and assignment, and elicits attribution only after the answer is committed. We instantiate the framework as BACKROOMBench, a verified testbed spanning controlled logic and competition mathematics, multiple skill conditions, single-agent and multi-agent settings, and diverse model families. Our evaluation reveals a pervasive provenance failure. Across models and domains, stated skill use often remains stable while causal reliance and signed utility vary, producing both silent uptake and performative use. Behavioral effects follow procedural content more reliably than displayed skill identity, whereas stated attributions respond strongly to artifact availability. Observational detectors based on direct skill-use claims, text mentions, trace similarity, and an LLM judge do not identify which decisions actually depend on the skill. In multi-agent systems, skill influence can survive communication even after its source is lost, while no-skill teams still name skills and sources that were never supplied. These findings establish the Reasoning Backroom as a general AI provenance problem whose audit requires intervention.

</details>


### [205] [INCLAIR: Inception-Based Longitudinal Clinical Anomaly Detection with Informed Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27487)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出INCLAIR框架，通过多历史上下文评分、聚合证据及生成自然语言解释，在有限专家监督下实现纵向临床档案中的异常检测，并在三个数据集上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床档案中的异常检测具有临床重要性但面临挑战：异常证据稀疏、患者病史长度不一以及专家解释成本高昂。

Method: 提出INCLAIR框架，基于剖面内可交换性假设，将完整均值子序列得分转化为U统计量形式，进行方差分解并采用不完全子集近似以控制计算复杂度；针对均值聚合对局部异常的衰减效应，引入验证选定的top-k池化策略。

Result: 在三个临床数据集上，INCLAIR一致优于最先进的方法；在纵向类固醇档案的案例研究中，其预测和解释与结合DNA分析的领域专家评估结果一致。

Conclusion: INCLAIR能够在有限专家监督下实现具有临床行动意义的异常检测。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in longitudinal clinical profiles is clinically important but difficult: abnormal evidence is often sparse, patient histories have unequal length, and expert explanations are costly. We propose INCLAIR, a framework that scores each observation against multiple historical contexts, aggregates evidence at the profile level, and generates grounded natural-language explanations under limited expert supervision. Under stated within-profile exchangeability assumptions, the complete mean subsequence score takes an order-$l$ U-statistic form, yielding a variance decomposition and an incomplete-subset approximation that controls combinatorial inference cost independently of profile length. The same analysis shows that mean aggregation attenuates localized anomalies by a factor set by the anomaly support and profile length, motivating validation-selected top-$k$ pooling. Across three clinical datasets, INCLAIR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. We further validate practical relevance through a case study on longitudinal steroid profiles, comparing INCLAIR's predictions and explanations against domain-expert assessments supported by DNA analysis. The results show that INCLAIR enables clinically actionable anomaly detection under limited expert supervision.

</details>


### [206] [MedLLM: An Open Medical Language Model at the Sub-Billion Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27490)
*Maxx Richard Rahman,Asim Ahmed,Mihan Mohagheghzadeh,Wolfgang Maass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedLLM，一个0.1B参数的开源医疗语言模型，通过三阶段开放流程训练。在上下文相关问答中接近7B模型水平，在知识回忆任务上超越所有基线，揭示了亚十亿参数规模下医疗能力随任务类型分化的独特模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源医疗大模型普遍集中在7B及以上参数规模，导致亚十亿（sub-billion）参数区间缺乏系统性的特征描述和研究。

Method: 采用完全开放的三阶段训练流水线：1）使用课程序列长度调度进行通用预训练；2）基于新发布的参考引导医疗语料库MedFineWeb进行领域微调；3）结合监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）进行偏好对齐微调。
